In this scenario, automated waste recycling (AWR) using deep learning methods can assist humans in optimal waste management. Recent deep learning approaches for AWR provide promising waste segmentation performance, however, these methods rely on large backbone networks that are inefficient for AWR systems and suffer from performance deterioration in cluttered scenes. To this end, an optimal waste segmentation network is introduced which effectively utilizes the spatial domain to capture localized structural dependencies and the spectral domain to efficiently extract global contextual relationships. This cascaded design allows the network to progressively leverage both local and global representations across complementary domains to highlight the semantic information necessary for effective segmentation of various waste objects. Furthermore, auxiliary feature enhancement module (AFEM) is introduced to enhance the target objects' boundaries and blob amplification for better segmentation in cluttered scenarios. Extensive experimentation on ZeroWaste-aug, ZeroWaste-f and SpectralWaste datasets reveals the merits of the proposed method.

</details>


### [69] [Revisiting Vehicle Color Recognition in Long-Tailed Surveillance Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13625)
*Vinícius Orrú,Bruno H. Foggiatto,Gabriel E. Lima,David Menotti,Rayson Laroca*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对监控系统中车辆颜色识别面临的严重类别不平衡问题，提出结合合成少数类数据增强（文本条件图像生成+图像条件颜色编辑）、现代视觉表示、损失重加权、学习率调度、颜色安全增强、前景感知预处理和集成融合的综合方法，在UFPR-VeSV数据集上实现94.6%微平均准确率和79.7%宏平均准确率，宏平均准确率较近期文献提升8.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实监控场景中车辆颜色分布存在严重不平衡，传统整体准确率不足以评估模型在稀有但操作相关的颜色类别上的性能，亟需针对类别不平衡的车辆颜色识别方法。

Method: 1) 使用RunDiffusion/JuggernautXL进行文本条件图像生成和Gemini 2.0 Flash进行图像条件颜色编辑两种现成生成策略进行少数类合成增强；2) 结合现代视觉表征、损失重加权、学习率调度、颜色安全增强、前景感知预处理和集成融合等多重技术。

Result: 最佳方法在UFPR-VeSV数据集上达到94.6%微平均准确率和79.7%宏平均准确率，宏平均准确率较近期文献提升8.2个百分点。人工错误分析表明多数剩余失败案例对人类标注者而言也存在视觉模糊性。

Conclusion: 合成数据增强能有效缓解车辆颜色识别中的类别不平衡问题，但在无约束监控图像中基于颜色的车辆识别存在固有局限性，许多错误源自视觉本身的歧义性。

Abstract: Vehicle color recognition is an important cue for vehicle identification in surveillance systems, especially when license plates are illegible due to low resolution, occlusion, motion blur, or poor illumination. However, real-world vehicle color distributions are highly imbalanced, making overall accuracy insufficient to assess performance on rare but operationally relevant colors. This paper presents a comprehensive study of vehicle color recognition under severe class imbalance using UFPR-VeSV, a challenging real-world surveillance dataset. We investigate synthetic minority-class augmentation through two off-the-shelf generative strategies: text-conditioned image generation with RunDiffusion/JuggernautXL and image-conditioned color editing with Gemini 2.0 Flash. The curated synthetic data are combined with modern visual representations, loss reweighting, learning-rate scheduling, color-safe augmentation, foreground-aware preprocessing, and ensemble fusion. The bestperforming approach achieves 94.6% micro accuracy and 79.7% macro accuracy, improving macro accuracy by 8.2 percentage points over recent literature. A manual error analysis further shows that many remaining failures are visually ambiguous even for human annotators, highlighting the practical limits of color-based vehicle identification in unconstrained surveillance imagery. The generated images and source code are publicly available at https://github.com/viniciusorru/vcr-synthetic

</details>


### [70] [Surflo: Consistent 3D Surface Flow Model with Global State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13644)
*Antoine Guédon,Shu Nakamura,Nicolas Dufour,Jiahui Lei,Ko Nishino,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Surflo通过将任意数量无位姿RGB图像压缩为K个潜在令牌（全局状态），并利用流匹配将噪声点独立传输到3D表面，实现了从全局潜在表征解码任意分辨率（数千到百万点）的定向3D表面点，同时引入推理时引导项抑制独立解码的不一致性，在表面指标上匹配或超越前馈基线，速度比优化方法快一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈重建模型未能利用几何的视角不变性：逐视角方法输出重叠且未对齐的点云，线性增加计算量；全局潜在方法则局限于固定的低分辨率输出。需要一种既能利用全局状态又能灵活解码任意分辨率的方法。

Method: 1) 将可变数量无位姿RGB视图压缩为K个潜在令牌（全局状态）；2) 通过流匹配将噪声点独立传输到3D表面，解码出定向表面点；3) 推理时引入基于光度梯度的引导项，在ODE积分过程中关联邻近点以抑制局部不一致性。

Result: 在表面指标上匹配或超越前馈基线方法；运行速度比需要数百视图的优化方法快一个数量级；是唯一结合全局潜在表征与任意分辨率解码的前馈方法。

Conclusion: Surflo成功将全局潜在表征与灵活的高分辨率解码结合，证明了通过流匹配从压缩全局状态解码任意密度3D表面的可行性，为前馈3D重建提供了新范式。

Abstract: Geometry is invariant to viewpoint, which makes any collection of images a redundant encoding of a single 3D state. Existing feed-forward reconstruction models fail to exploit this: per-view methods emit overlapping, unaligned pointmaps that grow linearly with input count, while global-latent methods commit to a fixed, low-resolution output. We introduce Surflo, which compresses a variable number of unposed RGB views into K latent tokens-one global state-and decodes oriented 3D surface points by independently transporting them from noise onto the surface via flow matching. This frees the output from any fixed grid or token budget: the same latent yields from a few thousand to a million points in a single forward pass. To suppress the local inconsistencies inherent to independent per-point decoding, an inference-time guidance term correlates nearby points by injecting a photometric gradient during ODE integration. Surflo matches or surpasses feed-forward baselines on surface metrics, runs an order of magnitude faster than optimization-based methods that require hundreds of views, and is the only feed-forward approach to combine a global latent with arbitrary-resolution decoding.

</details>


### [71] [World Tracing: Generative Pixel-Aligned Geometry Beyond the Visible](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13652)
*Hao Zhang,Mohamed El Banani,Jen-Hao Cheng,Paul Zhang,Yi Hua,Ben Mildenhall,Christoph Lassner,Narendra Ahuja,Gengshan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: World Tracing提出了一种生成式像素对齐几何表示方法，通过为每个输入像素预测有序的相机空间3D点堆栈（可见表面+遮挡表面），并基于扩散Transformer（WT-DiT）实现，在可见表面重建和完整几何生成上均优于现有深度估计器和图像到3D生成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像到3D方法在保真度和完整性之间存在权衡：深度估计器锚定于输入像素但仅止于可见表面，而图像到3D模型能生成完整形状但常与输入不对齐。作者旨在同时实现像素对齐的可见表面重建和遮挡几何的完整生成。

Method: 提出World Tracing表示：为每个输入像素预测一个有序的相机空间3D点堆栈，第一层为可见表面，后续层从前到后表示与遮挡表面的交点。实例化为WT-DiT（世界追踪扩散Transformer），将多个几何层视为独立的去噪token，通过分解注意力和全局注意力耦合。采用像素空间流匹配训练，并设计了混合噪声调度以平衡可见表面重建和遮挡几何生成。

Result: 在物体、场景和动态基准上，World Tracing在可见表面重建和完整几何生成上均优于深度预测器和图像到3D生成模型。同时保留了2D到3D的对应关系，支持文本驱动的3D场景编辑、几何条件的新颖视图视频合成，以及与纹理网格生成器的无缝集成。

Conclusion: World Tracing通过生成式像素对齐几何表示有效统一了可见表面重建和遮挡几何补全，在多个任务上达到SOTA，并具有良好的可扩展性和下游应用潜力。

Abstract: Image-to-3D methods often trade off faithfulness and completeness: depth estimators are anchored to input pixels but stop at the visible surface, while image-to-3D models generate complete shapes that are often misaligned with the input. We introduce World Tracing, a generative pixel-aligned geometry representation that predicts 3D points aligned with observed pixels while completing geometry beyond the visible surface. For each input pixel, World Tracing predicts an ordered stack of camera-space 3D points, where the first layer represents the visible surface and subsequent layers represent front-to-back intersections with occluded surfaces. We instantiate this representation with a world-tracing diffusion transformer, WT-DiT, which treats multiple geometry layers as separate denoising tokens coupled through factorized and global attention. WT-DiT is trained with pixel-space flow matching and a mixed noise schedule that balances visible-surface reconstruction with occluded-geometry generation. World Tracing achieves strong performance on visible-surface reconstruction and complete geometry generation across object, scene, and dynamic benchmarks, outperforming both depth predictors and image-to-3D generators. It also preserves 2D-to-3D correspondence, enabling text-driven 3D scene editing, geometry-conditioned novel-view video synthesis, and training-free integration with textured-mesh generators.

</details>


### [72] [Flex4DHuman: Flexible Multi-view Video Diffusion for 4D Human Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13655)
*Jen-Hao Cheng,Yipeng Wang,Hao Zhang,Gengshan Yang,Jenq-Neng Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Flex4DHuman是一种多视图视频扩散模型，仅需单目或稀疏多视图视频及相对相机位姿条件，即可生成同步密集多视图视频，并可直接用于下游4D高斯溅射重建，在DNA-Rendering和ActorsHQ上超越先前方法，且可泛化至动物类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于人体的动态4D重建方法依赖骨架、深度图、法线等显式几何先验，这些先验获取成本高且泛化性受限。本文旨在设计一种无需显式几何先验、仅需相对相机位姿条件即可从单目或稀疏多视图视频生成密集多视图视频的扩散模型，以降低动态4D内容创建的难度和门槛。

Method: 基于Wan 2.1 1.3B文本到视频模型，保留骨干架构；通过五轴位置编码（扩展时空RoPE，加入视图索引和连续SE(3)相对相机几何）编码相机和视图信息；采用三阶段课程训练（位姿跟随、灵活参考-目标视图生成、时间展开）；训练中使用干净的歷史目标视图token；添加多视图caption以实现测试时文本控制；结合现成的4D高斯溅射重建管线。

Result: 在DNA-Rendering和ActorsHQ数据集上，Flex4DHuman超越了之前的最优方法；经混合人类-动物训练后，同样的公式可泛化到动物类别。

Conclusion: Flex4DHuman为实现从随意单目视频进行可扩展的4D内容创建提供了实用方案，可应用于仿真、游戏、AR/VR和视频重拍等场景。

Abstract: We present Flex4DHuman, a multi-view video diffusion model that transforms a monocular or sparse multi-view video of a dynamic subject into synchronized dense multi-view videos using only relative camera-pose conditioning. Unlike prior human-centric methods that rely on skeletons, depth maps, normals, or rendered target-view geometry, Flex4DHuman requires no explicit geometry priors and instead conditions generation through relative camera-pose positional encoding. The generated videos can be directly ingested by downstream reconstruction pipelines to create dynamic 4D Gaussian splats. Built on the Wan 2.1 1.3B text-to-video model, Flex4DHuman preserves the backbone architecture and encodes camera and view information through a five-axis positional encoding that extends spatio-temporal RoPE with view indices and continuous SE(3) relative camera geometry. A three-stage curriculum progressively trains the model for pose following, flexible reference-to-target view generation, and temporal rollout. To support temporal rollout, we train with clean historical target-view tokens. We also add multi-view captions to enable test-time text control. Combined with an off-the-shelf 4D Gaussian Splatting stage, our framework lifts monocular static-camera videos into dynamic 4D Gaussian splats. Experiments on DNA-Rendering and ActorsHQ show that Flex4DHuman surpasses prior state-of-the-art methods, while the same formulation generalizes to animal categories after mixed human-animal training. These capabilities make Flex4DHuman a practical step toward scalable 4D content creation from casual monocular videos for simulation, gaming, AR/VR, and video re-shooting.

</details>


### [73] [SpatialClaw: Rethinking Action Interface for Agentic Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13673)
*Seokju Cho,Ryo Hachiuma,Abhishek Badki,Hang Su,Byung-Kwan Lee,Chan Hee Song,Sifei Liu,Subhashree Radhakrishnan,Seungryong Kim,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Min-Hung Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpatialClaw，一种无需训练的空间推理框架，通过将代码作为动作接口，让VLM驱动的智能体在状态化Python内核中逐步执行感知与几何操作，实现灵活组合和分析，在20个3D/4D空间推理基准上平均准确率59.9%，超越最近空间智能体11.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间推理智能体受限于动作接口设计：单次代码执行无法利用中间结果，结构化工具调用缺乏灵活组合能力。两者均难以应对开放、复杂的3D/4D空间推理任务，因此需要设计更灵活的动作接口。

Method: SpatialClaw采用代码作为动作接口，维护一个状态化Python内核，预加载输入帧和感知/几何原语，每个步骤由VLM根据所有先前输出编写一个可执行单元，支持灵活组合操作、根据中间观察动态调整策略。

Result: 在20个静态和动态3D/4D空间推理基准上，SpatialClaw平均准确率59.9%，超越最近空间智能体11.2个百分点，在6种VLM骨干上均有一致提升，无需基准或模型特定适配。

Conclusion: 代码作为动作接口能够有效增强VLM的空间推理能力，提供比单次执行和结构化工具调用更灵活的组合与自适应分析能力。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning, the ability to determine where objects are, how they relate, and how they move in 3D, remains a fundamental challenge for vision-language models (VLMs). Tool-augmented agents attempt to address this by augmenting VLMs with specialist perception modules, yet their effectiveness is bounded by the action interface through which those tools are invoked. In this work, we study how the design of this interface shapes the agent's capacity for open-ended spatial reasoning. Existing spatial agents either employ single-pass code execution, which commits to a full analysis strategy before any intermediate result is observed, or rely on a structured tool-call interface that often offers less flexibility for freely composing operations or tailoring the analysis to each task. Both designs offer limited flexibility for open-ended, complex 3D/4D spatial reasoning. We therefore propose SpatialClaw, a training-free framework for spatial reasoning that adopts code as the action interface. SpatialClaw maintains a stateful Python kernel pre-loaded with input frames and a suite of perception and geometry primitives, letting a VLM-backed agent write one executable cell per step conditioned on all prior outputs, enabling the agent to flexibly compose and manipulate perception results and adapt its analysis to both intermediate text and visual observations and the demands of each problem. Evaluated across 20 spatial reasoning benchmarks spanning a broad range of static and dynamic 3D/4D spatial reasoning tasks, SpatialClaw achieves 59.9% average accuracy, outperforming the recent spatial agent by +11.2 points, with consistent gains across six VLM backbones from two model families without any benchmark- or model-specific adaptation.

</details>


### [74] [RepWAM: World Action Modeling with Representation Visual-Action Tokenizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13674)
*Junke Wang,Qihang Zhang,Shuai Yang,Yiming Luo,Yujun Shen,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RepWAM提出了一种基于表征视觉-动作分词器的世界动作模型，通过语义化的视觉-动作潜在空间联合建模未来视觉状态与潜在动作，在真实与仿真机器人操控任务中表现优异，超越了传统基于重建的视频分词器方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）通常继承来自预训练视频生成模型的重建导向视频分词器，虽然能保持视觉保真度，但像素级重建对学习指令遵循的动态（即连接未来预测与机器人控制）提供的指导有限。

Method: 1) 训练一个表征视觉-动作分词器，将视觉输入映射为对齐的视觉和潜在动作标记；2) 预训练WAM联合建模未来视觉状态及其连接潜在动作，并融入语言指令；3) 通过真实机器人轨迹进行微调，实现闭环操控。

Result: 在真实世界操控任务和仿真基准上，RepWAM在多种操控场景中展现出强劲性能，消融实验证实了语义视觉-动作分词相比重建导向方案的优越性。

Conclusion: 表征视觉-动作分词作为世界动作模型的基础具有良好前景，是迈向通用机器人策略的重要一步。

Abstract: This work presents RepWAM, a representation-centric world action model (WAM) built on representation visual-action tokenizers. Existing WAMs typically inherit reconstruction-oriented video tokenizers from pretrained video generation models. Although these tokenizers preserve visual fidelity, pixel reconstruction alone provides limited guidance for learning instruction-following dynamics that connect future prediction with robot control. To address this, we explore a semantic visual-action latent space for representation-centric world action modeling. Specifically, we train a representation visual-action tokenizer that maps visual inputs into aligned visual and latent action tokens. We then pretrain our WAM to jointly model future visual states and the latent actions that connect them under language instructions, followed by adaptation to real robot trajectories for closed-loop manipulation. Experiments on real-world manipulation tasks and simulation benchmarks show that RepWAM delivers strong performance across diverse manipulation settings, while ablations highlight the value of semantic visual-action tokenization over reconstruction-oriented alternatives. These results establish representation visual-action tokenization as a promising foundation for world action models and a step toward generalist robot policies. Code and weights will be available at https://github.com/wdrink/RepWAM.

</details>


### [75] [Modality Forcing for Scalable Spatial Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13676)
*Bardienus Pieter Duisterhof,Deva Ramanan,Jeffrey Ichnowski,Justin Johnson,Keunhong Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Modality Forcing，一种简单可扩展的后训练方法，利用文本到图像（T2I）模型中的空间先验，通过为每种模态分配独立噪声级别，实现图像与稀疏深度数据的联合生成和深度预测。实验表明该方法具有可扩展性，大模型在更多图像数据上训练可产生更准确的深度，最强模型在单目深度估计上达到SOTA水平，AbsRel比现有联合生成模型降低57%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法虽能利用T2I模型的空间先验进行深度预测，但需要密集深度数据且流程复杂。本文旨在提出一种更简单、可扩展的方法，仅用稀疏深度数据即可实现高质量的联合图像-深度生成与深度预测。

Method: 提出Modality Forcing后训练方法：为图像和深度两种模态分配独立的噪声级别，使单个DiT模型能够支持任意排列的条件生成和联合生成；使用各模态独立的解码器，支持在稀疏真实深度数据上训练。

Result: 从零训练370M到3.3B参数的T2I模型，发现更大模型和更多图像数据产生更准确的深度预测；最强模型在单目深度估计上与SOTA方法竞争，AbsRel比现有联合生成模型降低57%。

Conclusion: 图像生成可作为可扩展的预训练目标用于空间感知任务，Modality Forcing有效继承了T2I预训练的可扩展性。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models contain rich spatial priors. Synthesizing photorealistic, cluttered scenes requires an understanding of geometry, including perspective and relative scale. Prior works adapt T2I models to leverage this prior for depth prediction, but they require dense depth data and involve complex recipes. We propose Modality Forcing, a simple, scalable post-training recipe for joint image-depth generation using a single DiT trained on sparse depth data. Modality Forcing enables conditional and joint generation of image and depth in any permutation by assigning separate noise levels per modality. Per-modality decoders let us train on sparse, real-world depth and achieve strong, generalizable depth prediction. We further show that Modality Forcing inherits the scalability of T2I pre-training: by training a set of T2I models from scratch (370M to 3.3B parameters), we find that larger models trained on more image data produce more accurate depth. Our strongest model is competitive with state-of-the-art monocular depth estimators and reduces AbsRel by 57% relative to existing joint image-depth generative models. These results provide strong evidence that image generation is a scalable pre-training objective for spatial perception. https://modality-forcing.github.io/

</details>


### [76] [From Seeing to Experiencing: Scaling Navigation Foundation Models with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.22028)
*Honglin He,Yukai Ma,Brad Squicciarini,Wayne Wu,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Seeing-to-Experiencing (S2E)框架，通过结合离线视频预训练和强化学习后训练，增强导航基础模型在真实城市环境中的交互能力和安全性，并引入Anchor-Guided Distribution Matching和Residual-Attention Module两项技术创新，以及NavBench-GS评估基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航基础模型仅基于离线数据训练，缺乏对动作后果的推理能力和反事实适应能力，在需要交互和安全行为的真实城市导航场景（如避障、避让行人）中存在显著局限性。

Method: 提出S2E框架：1) Anchor-Guided Distribution Matching策略用于离线预训练，通过anchor监督稳定学习并建模多样化运动模式；2) Residual-Attention Module用于强化学习，在不擦除预训练知识的前提下从仿真环境获取反应式行为。同时构建基于3D高斯泼溅真实场景重建的NavBench-GS端到端评估基准。

Result: 在NavBench-GS基准上系统评估了导航基础模型的泛化性和安全性，验证了S2E框架的有效性。

Conclusion: S2E框架有效融合了大规模离线视频预训练的泛化能力和仿真环境强化学习的交互能力，解决了导航基础模型在真实世界交互场景中的适应性难题。

Abstract: Navigation foundation models trained on massive web-scale data enable agents to generalize across diverse environments and embodiments. However, these models, which are trained solely on offline data, often lack the capacity to reason about the consequences of their actions or adapt through counterfactual understanding. They thus face significant limitations in real-world urban navigation, where interactive and safe behaviors, such as avoiding obstacles and moving pedestrians, are critical. To tackle these challenges, we introduce the Seeing-to-Experiencing (S2E) learning framework to scale the capability of navigation foundation models with reinforcement learning. S2E combines the strengths of pretraining on offline videos and post-training through reinforcement learning. It maintains the model's generalizability acquired from large-scale real-world videos while enhancing its interactivity through reinforcement learning in simulation environments. Specifically, we introduce two innovations: (1) an Anchor-Guided Distribution Matching strategy for offline pretraining, which stabilizes learning and models diverse motion patterns through anchor-based supervision; and (2) a Residual-Attention Module for reinforcement learning, which obtains reactive behaviors from simulation environments without erasing the model's pretrained knowledge. Moreover, we establish a comprehensive end-to-end evaluation benchmark, NavBench-GS, built on photorealistic 3D Gaussian Splatting reconstructions of real-world scenes that incorporate physical interactions. It can systematically assess the generalizability and safety of navigation foundation models.

</details>


### [77] [InterleaveThinker: Reinforcing Agentic Interleaved Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13679)
*Dian Zheng,Harry Lee,Manyuan Zhang,Kaituo Feng,Zoey Guo,Ray Zhang,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InterleaveThinker 是首个为现有图像生成器赋予交错生成能力的多智能体流水线，通过规划器代理和评论家代理实现文本-图像序列的自动生成，并采用 GRPO 强化学习和单步奖励策略优化生成轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成器在单图生成和编辑上表现出色，但由于架构限制无法实现交错生成（文本-图像序列），而这类能力在视觉叙事、引导和具身操作中至关重要。现有统一多模态模型在此任务上性能有限。

Method: 提出 InterleaveThinker 多智能体流水线：(1) 规划器代理组织图像-文本输入序列，为每步指定生成指令；(2) 评论家代理评估生成结果，识别偏离指令的样本并优化指令进行重生成。构建了 Interleave-Planner-SFT-80k 和 Interleave-Critic-SFT-112k 进行冷启动，再通过 Interleave-Critic-RL-13k 使用 GRPO 强化单步指令修正能力。提出准确率奖励和单步奖励，使单步 RL 能有效引导整个生成轨迹。

Result: InterleaveThinker 在多种图像生成器上均提升了交错生成性能，在交错生成基准上达到与 Nano Banana 和 GPT-5 相当的水平。同时显著提升了基模型在推理基准上的表现，例如在 4-step FLUX.2-klein 上的 WISE 和 RISE 指标均有大幅提升。

Conclusion: InterleaveThinker 有效解决了现有图像生成器无法进行交错生成的架构限制，通过多智能体协作和强化学习策略，不仅实现了高质量的交错生成，还意外地提升了基模型的推理能力。

Abstract: Recent image generators have demonstrated impressive photorealism and instruction-following capabilities in single-image generation and editing. However, constrained by their architectures, they cannot achieve interleaved generation (text-image sequence), which has crucial applications in visual narratives, guidance, and embodied manipulation. Even the latest open-source Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) exhibit limited performance in this regard. In this paper, we introduce InterleaveThinker, the first multi-agent pipeline designed to endow any existing image generator with interleaved generation capabilities. Specifically, we employ a planner agent to organize the image-text input sequence, instructing the image generator on the required execution at each step. Subsequently, we introduce a critic agent to evaluate the generator's outputs, identify samples that deviate from the planned instructions, and refine the instructions for regeneration. To implement this pipeline, we construct the Interleave-Planner-SFT-80k and Interleave-Critic-SFT-112k to perform a format cold-start. Then we develop Interleave-Critic-RL-13k to reinforce the step-wise instruction correction capability within a generation trajectory using GRPO. Since a single interleaved generation trajectory may involve over 25 generator calls, optimizing the entire trajectory is computationally impractical. Therefore, we propose accuracy reward and step-wise reward, allowing single-step RL to effectively guide the entire generation trajectory. The results show that InterleaveThinker improves performance across various image generators. On interleaved generation benchmarks, it achieves performance comparable to Nano Banana and GPT-5. Surprisingly, it also significantly enhances the base model on reasoning-based benchmarks; for example, on 4-step FLUX.2-klein, we observe substantial gains on WISE and RISE.

</details>


### [78] [GAE: Unleashing Physical Potential of VLM with Generalizable Action Expert](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.03896)
*Mingyu Liu,Zheng Huang,Xiaoyi Lin,Muzhi Zhu,Canyu Zhao,Yating Wang,Haoyi Zhu,Hao Chen,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出通用动作专家（GAE），通过稀疏几何接口将VLM的高层意图转换为连续机器人动作，并采用大规模预训练+轻量微调方案实现跨任务泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作方法将推理与动作生成纠缠在一起，导致泛化能力有限，需要解耦高层推理与低层动作执行。

Method: 提出稀疏几何接口：VLM预测稀疏3D路点作为高层意图，GAE将这些路点与实时点云观测映射为连续动作轨迹；提出APPF方案（动作预训练+点云微调），解耦动作动力学学习与几何接地；在15万条轨迹的大规模点云-轨迹数据集上预训练GAE。

Result: 在多样化视觉域、相机视角和自然语言指令下实现了强性能和良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过解耦推理与动作生成、引入稀疏几何接口以及两阶段预训练-微调方案，有效提升了机器人动作生成的泛化性。

Abstract: Vision-language models demonstrate strong reasoning and planning abilities, yet grounding these predictions into precise robot actions remains a central challenge. Existing Vision-Language-Action methods typically entangle reasoning and action generation, leading to limited generalization. We propose Generalizable Action Expert (GAE), a task-agnostic model that converts sparse geometric plans into dense robot actions. Our approach introduces a sparse geometric interface: the VLM predicts sparse 3D waypoints representing high-level intention, while GAE maps these waypoints together with real-time point cloud observations to continuous action trajectories. GAE is pretrained on a large-scale pointcloud-trajectory dataset comprising 150k trajectories from both simulation and real-world robots. To further improve efficiency and generalization, we introduce an Action Pre-training, Pointcloud Fine-tuning (APPF) scheme that decouples learning action dynamics from geometry grounding. After pretraining, GAE is frozen and reused across downstream tasks, requiring only lightweight fine-tuning of the VLM to produce the sparse interface. Experiments show that our method achieves strong performance and generalization across diverse visual domains, camera viewpoints, and natural language instructions.

</details>


### [79] [QueryOcc: Query-based Self-Supervision for 3D Semantic Occupancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.17221)
*Adam Lilja,Ji Lan,Junsheng Fu,Lars Hammarstrand*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: QueryOcc提出了一种基于查询的自监督框架，通过独立的4D时空查询直接从连续帧中学习3D语义占用，在自监督Occ3D-nuScenes基准上相比之前基于相机的方法提升了26%的语义RayIoU，运行速度达11.6 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模3D标注成本过高，现有自监督方法要么依赖2D渲染一致性（3D结构隐式学习），要么依赖来自累积激光雷达点云的离散体素网格（限制空间精度和可扩展性），亟需一种更高效、精确且可扩展的自监督3D语义占用学习方法。

Method: 提出QueryOcc框架，核心创新包括：(1) 使用独立4D时空查询在相邻帧间采样，直接学习连续3D语义占用；(2) 支持来自视觉基础模型的伪点云或原始激光雷达数据的监督；(3) 引入收缩场景表示（contractive scene representation），在常数内存下保留近场细节并压缩远场区域，实现长程监督与推理。

Result: 在自监督Occ3D-nuScenes基准上，QueryOcc相比之前基于相机的方法在语义RayIoU上提升26%，运行速度达11.6 FPS。

Conclusion: 直接4D查询监督能够实现强大的自监督占用学习，验证了该方法在3D场景几何与语义理解中的有效性，为自动驾驶中的自监督3D感知提供了新范式。

Abstract: Learning 3D scene geometry and semantics from images is a core challenge in computer vision and a key capability for autonomous driving. Since large-scale 3D annotation is prohibitively expensive, recent work explores self-supervised learning directly from sensor data without manual labels. Existing approaches either rely on 2D rendering consistency, where 3D structure emerges only implicitly, or on discretized voxel grids from accumulated lidar point clouds, limiting spatial precision and scalability. We introduce QueryOcc, a query-based self-supervised framework that learns continuous 3D semantic occupancy directly through independent 4D spatio-temporal queries sampled across adjacent frames. The framework supports supervision from either pseudo-point clouds derived from vision foundation models or raw lidar data. To enable long-range supervision and reasoning under constant memory, we introduce a contractive scene representation that preserves near-field detail while smoothly compressing distant regions. QueryOcc surpasses previous camera-based methods by 26% in semantic RayIoU on the self-supervised Occ3D-nuScenes benchmark while running at 11.6 FPS, demonstrating that direct 4D query supervision enables strong self-supervised occupancy learning. https://research.zenseact.com/publications/queryocc/

</details>


### [80] [Triangle Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31419)
*Nicholas Fry,Eric Dexheimer,Kirill Mazur,Paul H. J. Kelly,Andrew J. Davison*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个使用可微三角形（Triangle Splatting）作为3D地图表示的密集RGB-D SLAM系统，通过在线可微渲染实现跟踪与建图，并支持实时网格变形与碰撞检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯泼溅在新视角合成中表现优异，但三角形仍是传统渲染管线、游戏引擎及需要显式几何的下游任务（如仿真、碰撞、编辑）的标准基元。现有离线方法虽能通过Delaunay三角化从图像集优化出照片级网格，但缺乏在线SLAM能力。

Method: 采用可微三角形汤（Triangle Soup）作为地图表示，通过在线可微渲染同时进行跟踪与建图；利用受限Delaunay三角化将地图实时转换为连通网格，支持网格变形和碰撞检测。

Result: 在Replica和TUM-RGBD数据集上，3D几何重建优于基线方法，相机跟踪精度与现有方法持平，并实现了在线网格场景编辑。

Conclusion: 三角形泼溅可作为密集SLAM的有效地图表示，兼顾显式几何优势与可微优化能力，为在线几何编辑和物理交互等应用开辟新途径。

Abstract: We present a dense RGB-D SLAM system using differentiable triangles as the 3D map representation. While 3D Gaussian Splatting has emerged as the leading method for novel-view synthesis, triangles remain the standard primitive for traditional rendering hardware, game engines, and downstream tasks requiring explicit geometry such as simulation, collision, and editing. Recent offline methods have demonstrated that an unstructured 'triangle soup' can be optimised into a photorealistic mesh via Delaunay triangulation across a set of posed images. Building upon this insight, we present the first dense SLAM system to employ Triangle Splatting to perform both tracking and mapping through online differentiable rendering of a triangle soup. The map can be converted into a connected mesh on-the-fly via restricted Delaunay triangulation, enabling new online capabilities such as mesh deformation and collision checking. On Replica and TUM-RGBD, our system outperforms baselines on 3D geometry, matches the camera-tracking accuracy, and enables online mesh-based scene editing.

</details>


### [81] [Goal2Pixel: Grounding Goals to Pixels for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01621)
*Muyi Bao,Yuxin Cai,Hang Xu,Zongtai Li,Jinxi He,Jingfan Tang,Chen Lv,Ji Zhang,Yaqi Xie,Wenshan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Goal2Pixel提出了一种纯像素级的VLN-CE范 paradigm，将导航任务重新定义为可导航像素点定位，通过图像平面作为统一空间接口，结合可见性感知关键帧记忆和语义嵌入，在减少6倍VLM调用次数的同时实现了 competitive的导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM-based VLN-CE方法将导航建模为低层动作预测，存在动作接口模糊、受限于短视运动原语、以及因重复VLM查询导致效率低下等问题。

Method: 1) 将VLN-CE重新定义为可导航像素点定位，模型预测可见可导航像素点并反投影为3D航点；2) 在图像平面附加辅助指令区域（左/右/底部）分别对应左转、右转和停止；3) 提出可见性感知关键帧记忆机制实现紧凑且信息丰富的历史表示；4) 引入语义嵌入和坐标感知辅助损失以适配预训练VLM。

Result: R2R-CE Val-Unseen上达到54.1% SR和52.5% SPL，仅需每次episode 7.75次VLM调用，相比直接动作预测方法（46.62次调用、32.9% SR）减少了6倍调用次数。在RxR-CE上同样表现优异。

Conclusion: Goal2Pixel验证了纯像素级导航范 paradigm的有效性，通过将导航问题转化为像素定位而非动作预测，实现了更高效且高性能的连续环境视觉语言导航。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have become a common foundation for vision-and-language navigation in continuous environments (VLN-CE). Yet most VLM-based methods cast navigation as low-level action prediction, an interface that is ambiguous, tied to short-horizon motion primitives, and inefficient due to repeated VLM querying. We propose Goal2Pixel, a pure pixel-based paradigm that reformulates VLN-CE as navigable pixel grounding. Rather than predicting actions, Goal2Pixel uses the image plane as a unified spatial interface between VLM reasoning and robot motion: the model predicts a visible navigable pixel to the agent, which is back-projected into a 3D waypoint for forward navigation. For non-forward actions, we append auxiliary directive regions to the image plane, where the left/right/bottom regions are interpreted as turning left, turning right, and stopping, respectively. To enable long-horizon navigation, we propose a visibility-aware keyframe memory for compact and informative history representation. To adapt pretrained VLMs to navigable pixel grounding, we introduce semantic embeddings and coordinate-aware auxiliary losses. Goal2Pixel achieves competitive state-of-the-art performance while requiring fewer VLM inference calls than prior methods. On R2R-CE Val-Unseen it achieves 54.1% SR and 52.5% SPL with just 7.75 VLM calls per episode, 6x fewer than the 46.62 required by direct action prediction at 32.9% SR. The same trend holds on RxR-CE.Project Page: https://baobao0926.github.io/Goal2Pixel/.

</details>


### [82] [Radar-Guided Polynomial Fitting for Metric Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.17182)
*Patrick Rim,Hyoungseob Park,Vadim Ezhov,Jeffrey Moon,Alex Wong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: POLAR提出了一种基于多项式拟合的雷达引导深度估计方法，利用廉价雷达数据预测多项式系数，对预训练的单目深度估计模型输出的无尺度深度图进行非均匀变换，得到公制深度图，在三个数据集上实现了SOTA性能，MAE平均降低24.9%，RMSE降低33.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MDE模型虽然能推断合理的局部深度结构，但不同局部区域之间可能存在相对错位，导致简单的线性仿射变换不足以校正这种全局性偏差。已有方法依赖复杂架构或昂贵传感器，缺乏高效且通用的解决方案。

Method: 利用廉价雷达数据预测多项式系数，对MDE输出的无尺度深度图进行非均匀（多项式）变换，引入拐点以校正局部区域间的错位；同时提出基于一阶导数正则化的训练目标，保证局部单调性，维持结构一致性。

Result: 在三个数据集上达到SOTA，相比现有方法MAE平均降低24.9%，RMSE平均降低33.2%，同时在延迟和计算成本上也达到最优效率。

Conclusion: 多项式拟合框架有效泛化了仿射变换，能够通过雷达数据引导的非均匀校正解决局部深度错位问题，兼顾精度与效率，为雷达引导深度估计提供了新范式。

Abstract: We propose POLAR, a novel radar-guided depth estimation method that introduces polynomial fitting to efficiently transform scaleless depth predictions from pretrained monocular depth estimation (MDE) models into metric depth maps. Unlike existing approaches that rely on complex architectures or expensive sensors, our method is grounded in a fundamental insight: although MDE models often infer reasonable local depth structure within each object or local region, they may misalign these regions relative to one another, making a linear scale and shift (affine) transformation insufficient given three or more of these regions. To address this limitation, we use polynomial coefficients predicted from cheap, ubiquitous radar data to adaptively adjust predictions non-uniformly across depth ranges. In this way, POLAR generalizes beyond affine transformations and is able to correct such misalignments by introducing inflection points. Importantly, our polynomial fitting framework preserves structural consistency through a novel training objective that enforces local monotonicity via first-derivative regularization. POLAR achieves state-of-the-art performance across three datasets, outperforming existing methods by an average of 24.9% in MAE and 33.2% in RMSE, while also achieving state-of-the-art efficiency in terms of latency and computational cost.

</details>


### [83] [Iterative Tool Usage Exploration for Multimodal Agents via Step-wise Preference Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.21561)
*Pengxiang Li,Zhi Gao,Bofei Zhang,Yapeng Mi,Xiaojian Ma,Chenrui Shi,Tao Yuan,Yuwei Wu,Yunde Jia,Song-Chun Zhu,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SPORT方法，一种无需人工标注数据的多模态智能体迭代式工具使用探索方法，通过逐步偏好优化自动发现和优化工具使用策略，在GTA和GAIA基准上分别提升6.41%和3.64%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练多模态智能体的方法（监督微调、强化学习）依赖大量人工标注的任务-答案对和工具轨迹，对于复杂多模态任务而言，这种标注成本过高且难以获取。

Method: SPORT包含四个迭代组件：任务合成（利用语言模型合成多模态任务）、步骤采样（智能体尝试不同工具并获取结果）、步骤验证（使用验证器提供AI反馈构建逐步偏好数据）、偏好调优（利用偏好数据更新控制器）。通过四个组件的交替迭代，智能体在真实环境中自主进化。

Result: 在GTA基准上提升6.41%，在GAIA基准上提升3.64%，验证了方法的泛化性和有效性。

Conclusion: SPORT通过自探索和自优化消除了人工标注瓶颈，使多模态智能体无需预收集数据即可自主发现有效的工具使用策略，是一种通用且高效的多模态智能体训练方法。

Abstract: Multimodal agents, which integrate a controller e.g., a vision language model) with external tools, have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in tackling complex multimodal tasks. Existing approaches for training these agents, both supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, depend on extensive human-annotated task-answer pairs and tool trajectories. However, for complex multimodal tasks, such annotations are prohibitively expensive or impractical to obtain. In this paper, we propose an iterative tool usage exploration method for multimodal agents without any pre-collected data, namely SPORT, via step-wise preference optimization to refine the trajectories of tool usage. Our method enables multimodal agents to autonomously discover effective tool usage strategies through self-exploration and optimization, eliminating the bottleneck of human annotation. SPORT has four iterative components: task synthesis, step sampling, step verification, and preference tuning. We first synthesize multimodal tasks using language models. Then, we introduce a novel trajectory exploration scheme, where step sampling and step verification are executed alternately to solve synthesized tasks. In step sampling, the agent tries different tools and obtains corresponding results. In step verification, we employ a verifier to provide AI feedback to construct step-wise preference data. The data is subsequently used to update the controller for tool usage through preference tuning, producing a SPORT agent. By interacting with real environments, the SPORT agent gradually evolves into a more refined and capable system. Evaluation in the GTA and GAIA benchmarks shows that the SPORT agent achieves 6.41% and 3.64% improvements, underscoring the generalization and effectiveness introduced by our method. The project page is https://SPORT-Agents.github.io.

</details>


### [84] [Visual enhancement and 3D representation for underwater scenes: a review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.01869)
*Guoxi Huang,Haoran Wang,Brett Seymour,Evan Kovacs,John Ellerbroc,Dave Blackham,Nantheera Anantrasirichai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文全面综述了水下视觉增强（UVE）和水下3D重建技术，涵盖从传统方法到神经辐射场（NeRF）和3D高斯溅射（3DGS）等先进数据驱动技术，并对多种算法进行了定量和定性评估，最后指出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有众多水下增强算法，但缺乏同时涵盖水下视觉增强和水下3D重建的系统性综述，本研究旨在填补这一空白，推动水下视觉领域的发展。

Method: 首先介绍了水下成像的物理模型及其挑战，然后系统分类并综述了从非学习方法到数据驱动技术（包括NeRF和3DGS）的水下视觉增强与3D重建方法，最后在多个基准数据集上进行了定量和定性评估。

Result: 对多种主流水下视觉增强和3D重建算法进行了全面的定量与定性评估，比较了各方法在处理水下畸变方面的效果。

Conclusion: 总结了当前水下视觉领域的研究现状，指出了未来关键研究方向，为该领域的进一步探索提供了系统性的参考。

Abstract: Underwater visual enhancement (UVE) and underwater 3D reconstruction pose significant challenges in computer vision and AI-based tasks due to complex imaging conditions in aquatic environments. Despite the development of numerous enhancement algorithms, a comprehensive and systematic review covering both UVE and underwater 3D reconstruction remains absent. To advance research in these areas, we present an in-depth review from multiple perspectives. First, we introduce the fundamental physical models, highlighting the peculiarities that challenge conventional techniques. We survey advanced methods for visual enhancement and 3D reconstruction specifically designed for underwater scenarios. The paper assesses various approaches from non-learning methods to advanced data-driven techniques, including Neural Radiance Fields and 3D Gaussian Splatting, discussing their effectiveness in handling underwater distortions. Finally, we conduct both quantitative and qualitative evaluations of state-of-the-art UVE and underwater 3D reconstruction algorithms across multiple benchmark datasets. Finally, we highlight key research directions for future advancements in underwater vision.

</details>


### [85] [ReFoCUS: Reinforcement-guided Frame Optimization for Contextual Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01274)
*Hosu Lee,Junho Kim,Hyunjun Kim,Yong Man Ro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReFoCUS，首个将在线策略梯度强化学习集成到视频-LLM帧级优化的框架，通过自回归查询条件选择架构学习帧选择策略，无需显式帧级监督，在多个视频问答基准上持续提升推理准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频大语言模型在视频理解中受限于次优的帧选择策略，传统方法依赖静态启发式或外部检索模块，无法捕捉与用户查询相关的视觉线索，混淆了原始视觉动态与真实语义相关性。

Method: 采用在线策略梯度强化学习学习帧选择策略；利用参考模型生成的奖励信号来捕捉其在不同帧组合上的评分行为；采用自回归查询条件选择架构以降低组合空间复杂度；无需显式帧级监督，隐式发现最优语义一致帧组合。

Result: 在多个视频问答基准上持续提高了推理准确性，验证了将帧选择与模型内部效用对齐的优势。

Conclusion: ReFoCUS通过强化学习驱动的帧选择策略，有效对齐了帧选择与模型内部效用，为视频-LLM的帧级优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent progress in Large Multi-modal Models (LMMs) has enabled effective vision-language reasoning, yet the ability to video understanding remains constrained by suboptimal frame selection strategies, albeit with the rapid development of video-specialized LMMs. Prior works attempted to solve this with static heuristics or external retrieval modules to feed frame-level information, but these approaches often fail to capture visual cues grounded to the given user queries conflating raw visual dynamics with true semantic relevance. In this paper, we introduce ReFoCUS (Reinforcement-guided Frame Optimization for Contextual UnderStanding), the first framework to integrate online policy-gradient reinforcement learning into frame-level optimization for video-LLMs. ReFoCUS aims to learn a frame selection policy, leveraging reward signals derived from reference models to capture their underlying scoring behavior over frame combinations that best support temporally grounded responses. To efficiently explore the large combinatorial frame space, we employ an autoregressive and query-conditional selection architecture that ensures contextual consistency while reducing complexity. Our policy learning removes the need for explicit frame-level supervision, as it implicitly discovers optimal and semantically consistent frame compositions. ReFoCUS consistently improves reasoning accuracy across multiple video QA benchmarks, demonstrating the advantage of aligning frame selection with model-internal utility.

</details>


### [86] [CPAM: Context-Preserving Adaptive Manipulation for Zero-Shot Real Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.18438)
*Dinh-Khoi Vo,Thanh-Toan Do,Tam V. Nguyen,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CPAM，一种零样本的真实图像编辑框架，通过保留自适应模块和局部提取模块，结合掩码引导技术，实现复杂非刚性物体的纹理、身份和背景保持，在多个扩散骨干网络上表现优异，并在新构建的IMBA基准数据集上超越现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本引导的图像编辑方法在保持纹理和身份一致性、处理复杂非刚性物体、保留背景细节方面存在困难，且需要大量微调，缺乏零样本能力。

Method: 1) 保留自适应模块：调整自注意力机制以独立控制物体和背景，通过掩码引导技术保持物体形状、纹理、身份和背景不变；2) 局部提取模块：减少交叉注意力机制中非目标区域的干扰；3) 多种掩码引导策略：支持多样化的图像编辑任务。CPAM可无缝集成到SD1.5、SD2.1、SDXL等扩散骨干网络。

Result: 在自建的IMBA基准数据集上，CPAM在人工评估中成为首选，优于现有最先进的图像编辑技术，且在不同扩散模型架构上表现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: CPAM作为一种零样本框架，有效解决了复杂非刚性真实图像编辑中的一致性和保真度问题，具有良好的通用性和实用性，将开源代码和数据。

Abstract: Editing natural images using textual descriptions in text-to-image diffusion models remains a significant challenge, particularly in achieving consistent generation and handling complex, non-rigid objects. Existing methods often struggle to preserve textures and identity, require extensive fine-tuning, and exhibit limitations in editing specific spatial regions or objects while retaining background details. This paper proposes Context-Preserving Adaptive Manipulation (CPAM), a novel zero-shot framework for complicated, non-rigid real image editing. Specifically, we propose a preservation adaptation module that adjusts self-attention mechanisms to preserve and independently control the object and background effectively. This ensures that the objects' shapes, textures, and identities are maintained while keeping the background undistorted during the editing process using the mask guidance technique. Additionally, we develop a localized extraction module to mitigate the interference with the non-desired modified regions during conditioning in cross-attention mechanisms. We also introduce various mask-guidance strategies to facilitate diverse image manipulation tasks in a simple manner. CPAM can be seamlessly integrated with multiple diffusion backbones, including SD1.5, SD2.1, and SDXL, demonstrating strong generalization across different model architectures. Extensive experiments on our newly constructed Image Manipulation BenchmArk (IMBA), a robust benchmark dataset specifically designed for real image editing, demonstrate that our proposed method is the preferred choice among human raters, outperforming existing state-of-the-art editing techniques. The source code and data will be publicly released at the project page: https://vdkhoi20.github.io/CPAM

</details>


### [87] [ShowFlow: From Robust Single Concept to Condition-Free Multi-Concept Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.18493)
*Trong-Vu Hoang,Quang-Binh Nguyen,Thanh-Toan Do,Tam V. Nguyen,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ShowFlow框架，包含ShowFlow-S（单概念生成，使用KronA-WED适配器和语义感知注意力正则化SAR）和ShowFlow-M（多概念生成，复用ShowFlow-S模型，加入主题自适应匹配注意力SAMA和布局一致性引导），无需额外条件即可实现高保真身份保持和提示对齐的图像生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可控图像生成中，单概念场景下身份保持与提示对齐难以兼顾；多概念场景下仅依赖提示（无布局框或语义掩码等额外条件）会导致身份丢失和概念遗漏。

Method: 提出ShowFlow框架：ShowFlow-S引入KronA-WED适配器（Kronecker适配器+权重与嵌入分解）和新颖的语义感知注意力正则化（SAR）训练目标；ShowFlow-M直接复用ShowFlow-S训练好的模型，引入主题自适应匹配注意力（SAMA）和布局一致性引导作为即插即用模块，无需额外条件即可支持多概念生成。

Result: 大量实验和用户研究验证了ShowFlow的有效性，在广告、虚拟试衣等实际应用中展现出潜力。

Conclusion: ShowFlow通过解耦单概念与多概念生成，并设计轻量级适配器与注意力机制，有效解决了身份保持和概念完整性难题，为可控图像生成提供了通用解决方案。

Abstract: Customizing image generation remains a core challenge in controllable image synthesis. For single-concept generation, maintaining both identity preservation and prompt alignment is challenging. In multi-concept scenarios, relying solely on a prompt without additional conditions like layout boxes or semantic masks, often leads to identity loss and concept omission. In this paper, we introduce ShowFlow, a comprehensive framework designed to tackle these challenges. We propose ShowFlow-S for single-concept image generation, and ShowFlow-M for handling multiple concepts. ShowFlow-S introduces a KronA-WED adapter, which integrates a Kronecker adapter with weight and embedding decomposition, and together with a novel Semantic-Aware Attention Regularization (SAR) training objective to enhance single-concept generation. Building on this foundation, ShowFlow-M directly reuses robust models learned by ShowFlow-S to support multi-concept generation without extra conditions, incorporating a Subject-Adaptive Matching Attention (SAMA) and a Layout Consistency guidance as the plug-and-play module. Extensive experiments and user studies validate ShowFlow's effectiveness, highlighting its potential in real-world applications like advertising and virtual dressing. Our source code will be publicly available at: https://htrvu.github.io/showflow.

</details>


### [88] [Periodic-MAE: Periodic Video Masked Autoencoder for rPPG Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.21855)
*Jiho Choi,Sang Jun Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Periodic-MAE，一种基于掩码自编码器的自监督框架，通过学习未标记面部视频中周期性生理信号的时空表征，无需rPPG特定监督即可提升远程光电容积描记（rPPG）估计性能，在多个基准数据集和真实场景中均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有rPPG方法依赖大量有监督数据，泛化能力有限；而自监督方法未充分对齐rPPG信号的周期性特征。本文旨在设计一种无需rPPG标签即可学习通用且生理可解释时空表征的框架。

Method: 1）基于掩码自编码器（MAE）架构，重构被掩码的视频token；2）引入周期性感知的帧掩码策略，通过视频重采样使编码器学习准周期性时间模式；3）在MAE预训练中集成生理频带约束，利用脉冲信号在频域的稀疏性引导表征学习。

Result: 在PURE、UBFC-rPPG、MMPD、V4V四个公开数据集以及一个真实场景数据集上，Periodic-MAE持续提升rPPG估计性能，尤其在跨数据集和真实世界评估中优势显著。

Conclusion: 通过自监督预训练与生理先验（周期性掩码+频带约束）的有效结合，Periodic-MAE能够学习通用且可迁移的rPPG表征，提升模型在复杂场景下的鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose Periodic-MAE, a self-supervised framework for learning generalizable spatio-temporal representations of periodic physiological signals from unlabeled facial videos. The proposed method leverages a masked autoencoder (MAE), which learns high-dimensional facial representations by reconstructing masked video tokens without relying on remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) specific supervision. To explicitly align representation learning with the characteristics of rPPG, we introduce a periodicity-aware frame masking strategy based on video resampling, enabling the encoder to learn representations that capture quasi-periodic temporal patterns relevant to pulse signal estimation. In addition, physiological bandlimit constraints are integrated into the MAE pre-training framework, exploiting the sparsity of pulse signals in the frequency domain to guide the learned representations toward physiologically meaningful patterns. After pre-training, the learned representations are transferred to downstream rPPG estimation, where the encoder serves as a generic feature extractor for recovering pulse-related signals from facial videos. We conduct extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets, including PURE, UBFC-rPPG, MMPD, and V4V. Moreover, we evaluate the proposed approach on a real-world rPPG dataset collected under unconstrained lighting conditions and subject motion. Experimental results demonstrate that Periodic-MAE consistently improves rPPG estimation performance, particularly in challenging cross-dataset and real-world evaluation settings. Our code is available at https://github.com/ziiho08/Periodic-MAE.

</details>


### [89] [Modality-Aware Feature Matching in Visual and Vision-Language Applications: A Comprehensive Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.22791)
*Weide Liu,Wei Zhou,Jun Liu,Ping Hu,Jun Cheng,Jungong Han,Weisi Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该综述全面回顾了基于模态的特征匹配方法，涵盖传统手工方法和当代深度学习方法，涉及RGB图像、深度图像、3D点云、LiDAR扫描、医学图像和视觉-语言交互等多种模态，重点强调了模态感知的进步和跨模态应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征匹配是计算机视觉中的基石任务，广泛应用于图像检索、立体匹配、3D重建和SLAM等领域。随着数据模态日益多样化，传统方法难以应对显著模态间隙，亟需系统梳理不同模态下特征匹配方法的发展脉络与最新进展。

Method: 采用综述性研究方法，系统分类并比较了传统手工方法（如Harris角点检测器、SIFT和ORB描述子）与当代深度学习方法（如基于CNN的SuperPoint、基于Transformer的LoFTR等无检测器方法），并针对不同模态（RGB图像、深度图像、3D点云、LiDAR扫描、医学图像、视觉-语言交互）分别探讨了专用解决方案。

Result: 传统方法在中等程度内模态变化下表现鲁棒，但难以应对显著模态间隙；深度学习方法（尤其是无检测器策略）显著提升了跨模态的鲁棒性和适应性。模态感知的进步包括：深度图像的几何与深度专用描述子、3D点云的稀疏与密集学习方法、LiDAR扫描的注意力增强网络、医学图像的MIND描述子等。

Conclusion: 特征匹配正从传统单一模态向多模态和跨模态方向演进，尤其在医学图像配准和视觉-语言任务中展现出处理日益多样化数据交互的能力，为该领域未来发展提供了系统性参考。

Abstract: Feature matching is a cornerstone task in computer vision, essential for applications such as image retrieval, stereo matching, 3D reconstruction, and SLAM. This survey comprehensively reviews modality-based feature matching, exploring traditional handcrafted methods and emphasizing contemporary deep learning approaches across various modalities, including RGB images, depth images, 3D point clouds, LiDAR scans, medical images, and vision-language interactions. Traditional methods, leveraging detectors like Harris corners and descriptors such as SIFT and ORB, demonstrate robustness under moderate intra-modality variations but struggle with significant modality gaps. Contemporary deep learning-based methods, exemplified by detector-free strategies like CNN-based SuperPoint and transformer-based LoFTR, substantially improve robustness and adaptability across modalities. We highlight modality-aware advancements, such as geometric and depth-specific descriptors for depth images, sparse and dense learning methods for 3D point clouds, attention-enhanced neural networks for LiDAR scans, and specialized solutions like the MIND descriptor for complex medical image matching. Cross-modal applications, particularly in medical image registration and vision-language tasks, underscore the evolution of feature matching to handle increasingly diverse data interactions.

</details>


### [90] [Skeleton Sparsification and Densification Scale-Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21398)
*Julia Gierke,Pascal Peter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出骨架化尺度空间（skeletonisation scale-spaces）框架，通过稀疏化 medial axis 实现形状层次化简化，同时满足尺度空间关键属性（层次架构、可控简化、几何变换等变性），并引入致密化（densification）实现从粗到细的逆推，实验证明其在鲁棒骨架化、形状压缩和增材制造刚度增强中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Medial axis（中轴）对噪声敏感，边界微小变化会导致骨架不期望的扩张。传统剪枝方法虽能缓解，但缺乏尺度空间理论的支持，无法系统性地满足层次架构、可控简化和几何变换等变性等关键属性。

Method: 1) 提出骨架化尺度空间，通过 medial axis 的稀疏化实现形状层次化简化；2) 建立连续和离散形式的严格理论基础；3) 进一步提出致密化（densification）尺度空间，通过逐步生长骨架而非收缩，实现从粗到细的逆推，甚至生成超完备（overcomplete）形状表示。

Result: 通过概念验证实验展示了框架在鲁棒骨架化、形状压缩以及增材制造中刚度增强等实际任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 骨架化尺度空间结合了剪枝和尺度空间理论的优点，为形状分析提供了统一的理论框架，致密化则进一步扩展了应用范围，能够生成超越原始骨架的超完备表示。

Abstract: The Hamilton-Jacobi skeleton, also known as the medial axis, is a powerful shape descriptor that represents binary objects in terms of the centres of maximal inscribed discs. Despite its broad applicability, the medial axis suffers from sensitivity to noise: Minor boundary variations can lead to disproportionately large and undesirable expansions of the skeleton. Classical pruning methods mitigate this shortcoming by systematically removing extraneous skeletal branches. This sequential simplification of skeletons resembles the principle of sparsification scale-spaces that embed images into a family of reconstructions from increasingly sparse pixel representations.
  We combine both worlds by introducing skeletonisation scale-spaces: They leverage sparsification of the medial axis to achieve hierarchical simplification of shapes. Unlike conventional pruning, our framework inherently satisfies key scale-space properties such as hierarchical architecture, controllable simplification, and equivariance to geometric transformations. We provide a rigorous theoretical foundation in both continuous and discrete formulations and extend the concept further with densification. By growing the skeleton successively instead of shrinking it, we allow inverse progression from coarse to fine scales. Densification scale-spaces can even reach beyond the original skeleton to produce overcomplete shape representations with relevancy for practical applications.
  Through proof-of-concept experiments, we demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework for practical tasks including robust skeletonisation, shape compression, and stiffness enhancement for additive manufacturing.

</details>


### [91] [Self-Evolving Vision-Language Models for Image Quality Assessment via Voting and Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.25787)
*Wen Wen,Tianwu Zhi,Kanglong Fan,Yang Li,Xinge Peng,Yabin Zhang,Yiting Liao,Junlin Li,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EvoQuality是一个无需标注数据即可让视觉语言模型自主提升图像质量感知能力的框架。它利用自一致性原理生成伪排名标签，通过群体相对策略优化（GRPO）迭代优化模型，在7个IQA基准上超越现有监督方法，将零样本性能提升31.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM后训练依赖昂贵的人工标注数据，自监督方法在感知领域（如图像质量评估）尚未被探索，亟需一种无需标注即可提升VLM质量感知能力的方法。

Method: EvoQuality将自一致性原理适配到IQA的排序特性中：通过成对多数投票在VLM自身输出上建立质量排序共识作为伪标签，将这些伪排序构建为保真度奖励，利用GRPO进行迭代优化，使模型逐步提升感知能力。

Result: 在多个IQA基准上，零样本PLCC提升31.8%；在7个基准中的5个上超越有监督的VLM-based IQA模型；可堆叠到预训练IQA模型上增强泛化能力。

Conclusion: EvoQuality首次证明自监督学习可在感知领域有效替代人工标注，实现与监督方法竞争甚至更优的性能，且具有良好的灵活性和泛化性。

Abstract: Improving vision-language models (VLMs) in the post-training stage typically relies on supervised fine-tuning or reinforcement learning, methods that necessitate costly, human-annotated data. While self-supervised techniques have proven effective for enhancing reasoning capabilities, their application to perceptual domains such as image quality assessment (IQA) remains largely unexplored. In this work, we introduce EvoQuality, a novel framework that enables a VLM to autonomously refine its quality perception capabilities without any ground-truth labels. EvoQuality adapts the principle of self-consistency to the ranking-based nature of IQA. It generates pseudo-labels by performing pairwise majority voting on the VLM's own outputs to establish a consensus on relative quality. These pseudo-rankings are then formulated into a fidelity reward that guides the model's iterative evolution through group relative policy optimization (GRPO). By iteratively leveraging its own predictions, EvoQuality progressively refines the VLM's perceptual capability. Extensive experiments show that EvoQuality boosts the base VLM's zero-shot performance by 31.8% on PLCC across diverse IQA benchmarks. Remarkably, despite being entirely self-supervised, EvoQuality achieves performance that is competitive with, or even surpasses, state-of-the-art supervised VLM-based IQA models, outperforming these models on 5 out of 7 IQA benchmarks. Furthermore, the framework demonstrates significant flexibility, allowing it to be stacked with pre-trained IQA models to bolster generalization on unseen datasets. Codes and checkpoints will be available at https://github.com/bytedance/EvoQuality.

</details>


### [92] [Proto-LeakNet: Towards Signal-Leak Aware Attribution in Synthetic Human Face Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.04260)
*Claudio Giusti,Luca Guarnera,Sebastiano Battiato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Proto-LeakNet，一种利用扩散模型输出中残留信号泄漏（signal-leak）进行源归因和真实性验证的框架。该方法在潜在域中重新模拟部分前向扩散过程，结合时序注意力编码器和特征加权原型头，实现闭集分类与基于密度的开集评估，无需重新训练即可分析未见过的生成器，在多个基准上超越现有方法，Macro AUC达98.13%。代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成图像和深度伪造生成技术的日益成熟使得来源归因和真实性验证成为现代计算机视觉系统的关键挑战。现有研究指出扩散模型会在其输出中无意识地留下统计痕迹（signal-leak），本文利用这一现象构建可解释的归因框架。

Method: Proto-LeakNet包含三个核心组件：在扩散模型潜在域中重新模拟部分前向扩散过程以暴露残差生成器特定线索；时序注意力编码器聚合多步潜在特征；特征加权原型头构建结构化的嵌入空间，实现透明归因。同时结合闭集分类与基于密度的开集评估，无需重训练即可分析未见生成器。

Result: 仅使用闭集数据训练，Proto-LeakNet在真实图像与已知生成器之间、已知生成器与未见生成器之间均实现了强可分离性，Macro AUC达98.13%，在后处理下保持鲁棒性，超越当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 提出的信号泄漏感知原型网络框架有效解决了扩散模型生成图像的归因与检测问题，兼具高性能与可解释性，代码已开源。

Abstract: The growing sophistication of synthetic image and deepfake generation models has turned source attribution and authenticity verification into a critical challenge for modern computer vision systems. Recent studies suggest that diffusion pipelines unintentionally imprint persistent statistical traces, known as signal-leaks, within their outputs, particularly in latent representations. Building on this observation, we propose Proto-LeakNet, a signal-leak-aware and interpretable attribution framework that integrates Closed-set classification with a density-based Open-set evaluation on the learned embeddings, enabling analysis of unseen generators without retraining. Acting in the latent domain of diffusion models, our method re-simulates partial forward diffusion to expose residual generator-specific cues. A temporal attention encoder aggregates multi-step latent features, while a feature-weighted prototype head structures the embedding space and enables transparent attribution. Trained solely on closed data and achieving a Macro AUC of 98.13\%, Proto-LeakNet learns a latent geometry that remains robust under post-processing, surpassing state-of-the-art methods, and achieves strong separability both between real images and known generators, and between known and unseen ones. The codebase is available at the following link: https://github.com/claudiunderthehood/Proto-LeakNet .

</details>


### [93] [Navigating Gigapixel Pathology Images with Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.19652)
*Thomas A. Buckley,Kian R. Weihrauch,Katherine Latham,Andrew Z. Zhou,Padmini A. Manrai,Arjun K. Manrai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GIANT是一种无需训练的多模态方法，通过让通用模型自主导航病理全切片图像（WSIs），在多尺度下迭代选择视野并聚合证据，在五个病理问答基准的四个上达到SOTA，同时发布了包含934个问题和868张WSI的MultiPathQA基准套件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大规模病理切片对话系统高度特化，通常将WSI压缩为固定切片级嵌入，或依赖多组件流水线，导致丢失多尺度细节且泛化能力受限。缺乏一个简单、无需训练且能保留多尺度信息、具有良好泛化性的WSI问答方法。

Method: GIANT（Gigapixel Image Agent for Navigating Tissue）采用无需训练的框架，让通用多模态模型自主导航WSI，通过迭代方式选择多放大倍率的图像区域，并随时间累积证据进行推理，无需针对病理任务进行专门微调。

Result: 在五个临床挑战基准中的四个上取得SOTA性能，超越了专门为病理问答设计的模型。基于GPT-5实现。

Conclusion: GIANT证明了通用多模态模型通过合理的导航策略即可在病理WSI问答任务上超越专用模型，无需训练或复杂流水线。MultiPathQA基准为病理问答的泛化性和可重复性提供了标准化评估平台。

Abstract: Recent advances in large multimodal models have allowed for the development of interactive chat models that can converse and reason about pathology whole-slide images (WSIs). However, existing slide-level chat systems are often highly specialized, typically compressing WSIs into fixed slide-level embeddings or relying on multi-component pipelines, which can lose multi-scale detail and limit generalizability beyond the target task. We present GIANT (Gigapixel Image Agent for Navigating Tissue), a simple, training-free approach that lets general-purpose multimodal models navigate WSIs on their own, iteratively selecting multi-magnification crops and aggregating evidence over time. To evaluate generalizability in WSI question answering and to promote reproducibility, we introduce MultiPathQA, a benchmark suite spanning five clinical challenges and 934 questions over 868 unique WSIs. This includes a new set of 128 pathologist-authored multiple-choice questions designed to mirror real diagnostic search and multi-scale reasoning. Using GPT-5, GIANT outperforms models specialized for pathology question answering, achieving state-of-the-art performance on four out of five benchmarks.

</details>


### [94] [Measurement Plasticity: Sensor-Level Adaptation for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.12571)
*Boyeong Im,Wooseok Lee,Yoojin Kwon,Hyung-Sin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MVP框架，将相机曝光三角（ISO、快门速度、光圈）作为物理提示，通过选择-投票机制实现测试时适应，在ImageNet-ES上优于纯数字TTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将TTA从数字域（tokens）扩展到物理域（photons），利用相机曝光参数作为可调节的物理提示，解决传统TTA方法需要梯度或模型修改的问题。

Method: 1) 使用源亲和度分数选择多个物理视图；2) 对每个保留视图进行数字增强变体评估，过滤最低熵预测；3) 通过硬投票聚合预测。整个流程为前向计算，无需梯度或模型修改。

Result: 在ImageNet-ES和ImageNet-ES-Diverse上，MVP在自动曝光和结合传统传感器控制场景下均优于纯数字TTA方法；在减少参数候选项降低捕获延迟时仍保持有效性。

Conclusion: MVP是一种简单、校准友好、实用的物理提示TTA框架，验证了从数字域向物理域扩展TTA的可行性。

Abstract: We propose Multi-View Physical-prompt (MVP) for Test-Time Adaptation (TTA), a forward-only framework that moves TTA from tokens to photons by treating the camera exposure triangle (i.e., ISO, shutter speed, and aperture) as physical prompts. At inference, MVP acquires selected multiple physical views using a source-affinity score, evaluates digitally augmented variants of each retained view and filters the lowest-entropy predictions, and aggregates predictions with hard voting. This selection-then-vote design is simple, calibration-friendly, and requires no gradients or model modifications. On ImageNet-ES and ImageNet-ES-Diverse, MVP outperforms digital-only TTA on both Auto-Exposure and a combination with conventional sensor control. MVP remains effective under reduced parameter candidates that lower capture latency, demonstrating its practicality.

</details>


### [95] [Adaptable Segmentation Pipeline for Diverse Brain Tumors with Radiomic-Guided Subtyping and Lesion-Wise Model Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.14648)
*Daniel Capellán-Martín,Abhijeet Parida,Zhifan Jiang,Nishad Kulkarni,Krithika Iyer,Austin Tapp,Syed Muhammad Anwar,María J. Ledesma-Carbayo,Marius George Linguraru*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种灵活、模块化、可适配的脑肿瘤分割流程，通过组合多模型、肿瘤特异性预处理/后处理以及影像组学特征分析，在BraTS 2025 Lighthouse Challenge多个数据集上取得了与顶尖算法相当的性能，无需依赖特定网络架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多参数MRI脑肿瘤分割的鲁棒性和泛化性仍然困难，因为肿瘤类型差异很大。BraTS 2025 Lighthouse Challenge通过涵盖成人/儿童、脑膜瘤、转移瘤等多种高质量数据集对分割方法进行基准测试，需要能够应对肿瘤异质性的通用解决方案。

Method: 提出灵活、模块化、可适配的流程：(1) 选择并组合多个最先进模型；(2) 在训练前后应用肿瘤和病灶特异性处理；(3) 从MRI中提取影像组学特征检测肿瘤亚型，实现更均衡训练；(4) 使用自定义病灶级性能指标确定集成中各模型的权重并优化后处理，实现每一步的个案定制。

Result: 在BraTS测试集上，该方法在多个挑战任务中取得了与顶级算法相当的性能。

Conclusion: 自定义病灶感知处理和模型选择能够产生鲁棒的分割结果，且不依赖特定网络架构。该方法有望在临床实践中支持肿瘤定量测量、诊断和预后评估。

Abstract: Robust and generalizable segmentation of brain tumors on multi-parametric magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) remains difficult because tumor types differ widely. The BraTS 2025 Lighthouse Challenge benchmarks segmentation methods on diverse high-quality datasets of adult and pediatric tumors: multi-consortium international pediatric brain tumor segmentation (PED), preoperative meningioma tumor segmentation (MEN), meningioma radiotherapy segmentation (MEN-RT), and segmentation of pre- and post-treatment brain metastases (MET). We present a flexible, modular, and adaptable pipeline that improves segmentation performance by selecting and combining state-of-the-art models and applying tumor- and lesion-specific processing before and after training. Radiomic features extracted from MRI help detect tumor subtype, ensuring a more balanced training. Custom lesion-level performance metrics determine the influence of each model in the ensemble and optimize post-processing that further refines the predictions, enabling the workflow to tailor every step to each case. On the BraTS testing sets, our pipeline achieved performance comparable to top-ranked algorithms across multiple challenges. These findings confirm that custom lesion-aware processing and model selection yield robust segmentations yet without locking the method to a specific network architecture. Our method has the potential for quantitative tumor measurement in clinical practice, supporting diagnosis and prognosis.

</details>


### [96] [Improving Pre-trained Adult Glioma Segmentation Models Using only Post-processing Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.14937)
*Abhijeet Parida,Daniel Capellán-Martín,Zhifan Jiang,Nishad Kulkarni,Krithika Iyer,Austin Tapp,Syed Muhammad Anwar,María J. Ledesma-Carbayo,Marius George Linguraru*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应后处理技术，用于优化大规模预训练模型在胶质瘤MRI分割中的输出质量，在BraTS 2025挑战中使撒哈拉以南非洲任务提升14.9%，成人胶质瘤任务提升0.9%，倡导从复杂模型架构转向高效、临床对齐的后处理策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练模型在胶质瘤mpMRI分割中泛化能力差，易产生假阳性、标签交换、切片不连续等系统性错误，且受限于GPU资源不均和模型训练的环境成本。

Method: 提出自适应后处理技术，用于优化各类肿瘤预训练模型输出的胶质瘤分割质量，替代追求更复杂模型架构的思路。

Result: 在BraTS 2025挑战中，撒哈拉以南非洲任务排名指标提升14.9%，成人胶质瘤任务提升0.9%。

Conclusion: 脑肿瘤分割研究应从日益复杂的模型架构转向高效、临床对齐、计算公平且可持续的后处理策略。

Abstract: Gliomas are the most common malignant brain tumors in adults and are among the most lethal. Despite aggressive treatment, the median survival rate is less than 15 months. Accurate multiparametric MRI (mpMRI) tumor segmentation is critical for surgical planning, radiotherapy, and disease monitoring. While deep learning models have improved the accuracy of automated segmentation, large-scale pre-trained models generalize poorly and often underperform, producing systematic errors such as false positives, label swaps, and slice discontinuities in slices. These limitations are further compounded by unequal access to GPU resources and the growing environmental cost of large-scale model training. In this work, we propose adaptive post-processing techniques to refine the quality of glioma segmentations produced by large-scale pretrained models developed for various types of tumors. We demonstrated the techniques in multiple BraTS 2025 segmentation challenge tasks, with the ranking metric improving by 14.9 % for the sub-Saharan Africa challenge and 0.9% for the adult glioma challenge. This approach promotes a shift in brain tumor segmentation research from increasingly complex model architectures to efficient, clinically aligned post-processing strategies that are precise, computationally fair, and sustainable.

</details>


### [97] [Why Commodity WiFi Sensors Fail at Multi-Person Gait Identification: A Systematic Analysis Using ESP32](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.02177)
*Oliver Custance,Saad Khan,Simon Parkinson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过在ESP32商用WiFi设备上进行系统实证研究，评估六种信号分离方法在1-10人场景下的多人员步态识别性能。研究发现所有方法准确率仅为39%-56%，特征空间重叠度高达97%-99%，表明多人员步态识别的瓶颈主要在于商用WiFi硬件感知质量而非算法选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WiFi CSI在单人步态识别上表现良好，但多人员场景下的可行性存疑。核心问题是：多人员识别性能低下主要是算法限制，还是商用WiFi硬件存在更根本的感知天花板？

Method: 使用ESP32商用WiFi传感器，在1-10人的七种场景下评估六种信号分离方法（FastICA、SOBI、PCA-ICA、NMF、小波变换、张量分解），并引入三种诊断指标：个体内变异性(ISV)、个体间可区分性(ISD)和性能退化率(PDR)。

Result: 所有方法准确率仅39%-56%，NMF表现最佳达56%；特征空间重叠度97%-99%；个体内表征不稳定；环境敏感性显著。算法选择对性能改善有限。

Conclusion: 在商用ESP32 WiFi CSI约束下，密集多人员步态识别的主要瓶颈是硬件感知质量和空间多样性，而非分离算法。这对安全与隐私有重要启示：商用WiFi CSI作为多用户生物识别身份认证的实用性值得怀疑，同时也限定了低成本WiFi硬件的被动识别能力上限。

Abstract: WiFi Channel State Information (CSI) has shown promise for single-person gait identification, raising interest in its use for contactless biometrics, continuous authentication, and passive identification. However, the feasibility of multi-person identification on low-cost commodity devices remains unclear. A critical question is whether weak multi-person performance is primarily an algorithmic limitation, or whether it reflects a more fundamental sensing ceiling on commodity WiFi hardware. We address this question through a systematic empirical study using commodity ESP32 WiFi sensors. We evaluated six different signal separation methods--FastICA, SOBI, PCA-ICA, NMF, Wavelet, and Tensor decomposition--across seven scenarios spanning 1-10 people in both controlled and realistic indoor environments. To investigate beyond classification accuracy, we introduce three diagnostic metrics: intra-subject variability (ISV), inter-subject distinguishability (ISD), and performance degradation rate (PDR). In all methods, performance remains moderate (39%-56% accuracy), with limited evidence that algorithmic choice alone solves the problem. The best-performing method, NMF, reaches 56% accuracy, while all methods exhibit extremely high feature-space overlap (97%-99%), unstable within-subject representations, and marked environmental sensitivity. These findings suggest that, under commodity ESP32 CSI constraints, dense multi-person gait identification is limited more by sensing quality and spatial diversity than by the chosen separation algorithm. Our results have direct implications for security and privacy: they call into question the practicality of commodity WiFi CSI as a robust multi-user biometric primitive for authentication, while also placing important bounds on the passive identification capabilities achievable with low-cost off-the-shelf WiFi hardware.

</details>


### [98] [EyeTheia: A Lightweight and Accessible Eye-Tracking Toolbox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06279)
*Stevenson Pather,Niels Martignène,Arnaud Bugnet,Fouad Boutaleb,Fabien D'Hondt,Deise Santana Maia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EyeTheia是一个轻量级、开源的深度学习管道，利用标准笔记本电脑摄像头实现实时网络摄像头眼动追踪，结合MediaPipe特征提取和CNN架构，支持用户特定微调，在MPIIFaceGaze验证和Dot-Probe任务中表现良好，与商业追踪器SeeSo SDK具有高度一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有眼动追踪方案要么依赖昂贵硬件设备，要么使用商业闭源软件，缺乏低成本、透明、可扩展的解决方案，难以在浏览器实验平台和真实临床研究中大规模部署。

Method: 采用MediaPipe进行面部关键点提取，结合基于iTracker启发的卷积神经网络进行目光估计；探索两种策略：基于移动数据预训练模型迁移学习和在桌面数据集上从头训练；支持用户特定微调以降低预测误差。

Result: 在MPIIFaceGaze数据集上，两种策略在校准前性能相当；用户特定微调一致性地降低了目光预测误差。在Dot-Probe任务中与SeeSo SDK对比，左右目光分配具有强一致性，但时间变异性更高。

Conclusion: EyeTheia提供了一个透明、可扩展、低成本的网络摄像头眼动追踪方案，适用于可重复的实验和临床研究，代码和模型已公开。

Abstract: We introduce EyeTheia, a lightweight and open deep learning pipeline for webcam-based gaze estimation, designed for browser-based experimental platforms and real-world cognitive and clinical research. EyeTheia enables real-time gaze tracking using only a standard laptop webcam, combining MediaPipe-based landmark extraction with a convolutional neural network inspired by iTracker and optional user-specific fine-tuning. We investigate two complementary strategies: adapting a model pretrained on mobile data and training the same architecture from scratch on a desktop-oriented dataset. Validation results on MPIIFaceGaze show comparable performance between both approaches prior to calibration, while lightweight user-specific fine-tuning consistently reduces gaze prediction error. We further evaluate EyeTheia in a realistic Dot-Probe task and compare it to the commercial webcam-based tracker SeeSo SDK. Results indicate strong agreement in left-right gaze allocation during stimulus presentation, despite higher temporal variability. Overall, EyeTheia provides a transparent and extensible solution for low-cost gaze tracking, suitable for scalable and reproducible experimental and clinical studies. The code, trained models, and experimental materials are publicly available.

</details>


### [99] [VDE Bench: Evaluating The Capability of Image Editing Models to Modify Visual Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00122)
*Hongzhu Yi,Yujia Yang,Yuanxiang Wang,Tong Li,Zhenyu Guan,Tianyu Zong,Jiahuan Chen,Chenxi Bao,Tiankun Yang,Haopeng Jin,Yixuan Yuan,Xinming Wang,Tao Yu,Ruilin Gao,Ruiwen Tao,Haijin Liang,Jin Ma,Jinwen Luo,Yeshani,Xinyu Zuo,Jungang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了VDE Bench，一个针对双语（中英）密集视觉文档图像编辑任务的人工标注基准，包含942个指令编辑样本和基于OCR解析的细粒度评估框架，系统评估了现有图像编辑模型在密集文档场景下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑模型主要针对英文和稀疏文本场景，无法有效处理密集、结构复杂的文档以及中文等非拉丁文字，这一重要方向尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 构建了包含942个指令编辑样本的高质量数据集，种子图像涵盖学术论文、海报、幻灯片、考试材料和报纸等中英文密集文档；同时提出了基于OCR解析层级的自动化评估框架，实现文本修改精度的细粒度量化。

Result: 基于该基准对代表性图像编辑模型进行了全面评估，人工验证表明人工判断与自动评估指标之间存在高度一致性。

Conclusion: VDE Bench是首个系统评估图像编辑模型在双语密集文本视觉文档上性能的基准，填补了该领域的研究空白。

Abstract: In recent years, image editing models have made significant progress, enabling users to manipulate visual content in a flexible and interactive manner through natural language instructions. However, an important yet underexplored research direction remains dense visual document image editing, which involves modifying textual content within images while faithfully preserving the original text style and background context. Existing methods primarily focus on English scenarios and images with relatively sparse text, and thus cannot adequately address dense, structurally complex documents or non-Latin scripts such as Chinese. To bridge this gap, we propose VDE Bench (Visual Doc Edit Bench), a rigorously human annotated and evaluated benchmark specifically designed to assess the performance of image editing models on bilingual Chinese-English and complex visual document editing tasks. The benchmark comprises a high quality dataset of 942 instruction based image editing samples, whose seed images encompass dense Chinese and English text documents including academic papers, posters, presentation slides, examination materials, and newspapers. Furthermore, we introduce a novel evaluation framework that systematically quantifies editing performance at the OCR parsing level, thereby enabling fine grained assessment of text modification accuracy. Based on this benchmark, we conduct a comprehensive evaluation of representative image editing models. Human verification demonstrates a high degree of consistency between human judgments and automated evaluation metrics. VDE Bench constitutes the first systematic benchmark for evaluating the performance of image editing models on bilingual dense text visual documents.

</details>


### [100] [LatentLens: Revealing Highly Interpretable Visual Tokens in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00462)
*Benno Krojer,Shravan Nayak,Oscar Mañas,Vaibhav Adlakha,Desmond Elliott,Siva Reddy,Marius Mosbach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LatentLens方法，通过将视觉token表示与大型文本语料库中的上下文token表示进行最近邻匹配，实现了对视觉语言模型中视觉token的可解释性分析。实验表明，相比LogitLens，LatentLens能更准确揭示视觉token的语义信息，且大多数视觉token在所有层都是可解释的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解为何LLM能够通过简单的浅层MLP映射处理视觉token，需要可解释性方法来揭示视觉token在每一层LLM处理中的编码内容。

Method: LatentLens方法：编码大型文本语料库，存储每个token的上下文表示；然后将视觉token表示与这些上下文表示进行最近邻匹配，将最相似的邻居表示作为视觉token的描述。

Result: 在15种不同VLM上评估，LatentLens表明LogitLens等常用方法严重低估了视觉token的可解释性。使用LatentLens，大多数视觉token在所有研究的模型和所有层中都是可解释的。

Conclusion: LatentLens产生的描述具有语义意义，比单个token提供更细粒度的人类可理解解释。研究结果提供了视觉与语言表征对齐的新证据，为分析LLM的潜在表征开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Transforming a large language model (LLM) into a vision-language model (VLM) can be achieved by mapping the visual tokens from a vision encoder into the embedding space of an LLM. Intriguingly, this mapping can be as simple as a shallow MLP transformation. To understand why LLMs can so readily process visual tokens, we need interpretability methods that reveal what is encoded in the visual token representations at every layer of LLM processing. In this work, we introduce LatentLens, a novel approach for mapping latent representations to descriptions in natural language. LatentLens encodes a large text corpus and stores contextualized token representations for each token in that corpus. Visual token representations are then compared to these contextualized representations and the top-nearest neighbor representations serve as descriptions of the visual token. We evaluate this method on 15 different VLMs, showing that commonly used methods, such as LogitLens, substantially underestimate the interpretability of visual tokens. With LatentLens instead, the majority of visual tokens are interpretable across all studied models and all layers. Qualitatively, we show that the descriptions produced by LatentLens are semantically meaningful and provide more fine-grained interpretations for humans compared to individual tokens. More broadly, our findings contribute new evidence on the alignment between vision and language representations and open up new directions for analyzing the latent representations of LLMs.

</details>


### [101] [Ex-Omni: Enabling 3D Facial Animation Generation for Omni-modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.07106)
*Haoyu Zhang,Zhipeng Li,Yiwen Guo,Tianshu Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Ex-Omni是一个开源模型，通过解耦语义推理与时间生成、引入blendshape感知的语音单元生成器与解码器、统一标记查询门控融合（TQGF）机制以及InstructS2SF-1200K数据集，实现了大语言模型原生支持带语音的3D面部动画生成，在保持语音理解与生成能力的同时，实现了更优的视听同步和更低的面部生成延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全模态大语言模型在统一多模态理解与生成方面取得了进展，但联合生成语音与3D面部动画这一对自然交互至关重要的任务尚未被充分探索。核心挑战在于LLM的离散语义推理与3D面部运动所需的密集时间动态之间存在不匹配。

Method: 1) 通过blendshape感知的语音单元生成器和blendshape解码器，将语义推理与时间生成解耦；2) 语音单元提供时间支架，隐藏语音表征携带面部相关线索；3) 提出统一标记查询门控融合（TQGF）机制实现可控语义注入；4) 构建InstructS2SF-1200K数据集（1200K样本）用于预训练。

Result: Ex-Omni在保持有竞争力的语音理解与生成能力的同时，相比级联流水线实现了更好的视听同步和更低的面部生成延迟。

Conclusion: Ex-Omni成功将语音伴随的3D面部动画生成能力原生融入全模态大语言模型，为自然人机交互提供了更优的技术方案。

Abstract: Omni-modal large language models (OLLMs) aim to unify multimodal understanding and generation, yet extending them to jointly produce speech and 3D facial animation remains largely unexplored despite its importance for natural human-computer interaction. A key challenge is the mismatch between the discrete semantic reasoning of LLMs and the dense temporal dynamics required for 3D facial motion. We propose Expressive Omni (Ex-Omni), an open-source model that augments OLLMs with native speech-accompanied 3D facial animation. Ex-Omni decouples semantic reasoning from temporal generation through a blendshape-aware speech unit generator and a blendshape decoder, where speech units provide temporal scaffolding and hidden speech representations carry facially relevant cues. We further introduce a unified token-as-query gated fusion (TQGF) mechanism for controlled semantic injection, as well as InstructS2SF-1200K, a dataset consisting of 1200K samples for pre-training. Extensive experiments show that Ex-Omni maintains competitive speech understanding and generation ability while achieving better audio-visual synchronization and lower face-generation latency than cascaded pipelines.

</details>


### [102] [Allure of Craquelure: A Variational-Generative Approach to Crack Detection in Paintings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.09730)
*Laura Paul,Holger Rauhut,Martin Burger,Samira Kabri,Tim Roith*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种混合方法，将裂缝检测建模为逆问题，利用深度生成模型作为先验，结合Mumford-Shah变分泛函和裂缝先验，通过联合优化实现数字化绘画中裂缝的像素级自动检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动化裂缝检测因复杂画面及裂缝与画笔笔触、毛发等艺术特征的视觉相似性而极具挑战性，亟需能够区分裂缝和类似裂缝艺术特征的有效方法，以支持艺术品的数字化文档和修复工作。

Method: 将裂缝检测建模为逆问题，将观测图像分解为无裂缝绘画和裂缝分量；使用深度生成模型作为底层艺术作品的强先验，同时采用Mumford-Shah型变分泛函结合裂缝先验来捕捉裂缝结构，通过联合优化得到像素级裂缝定位图。

Result: 能够实现像素级的裂缝定位，有效区分裂缝与笔触、毛发等相似视觉特征。

Conclusion: 提出的混合方法结合了深度生成模型与变分方法的优势，为数字化绘画中的裂缝检测提供了一种有效的自动化解决方案，有助于艺术品的评估、文档记录和修复指导。

Abstract: Recent advances in imaging technologies, deep learning and numerical performance have enabled non-invasive detailed analysis of artworks, supporting their documentation and conservation. In particular, automated detection of craquelure in digitized paintings is crucial for assessing degradation and guiding restoration, yet remains challenging due to the possibly complex scenery and the visual similarity between cracks and crack-like artistic features such as brush strokes or hair. We propose a hybrid approach that models crack detection as an inverse problem, decomposing an observed image into a crack-free painting and a crack component. A deep generative model is employed as powerful prior for the underlying artwork, while crack structures are captured using a Mumford--Shah-type variational functional together with a crack prior. Joint optimization yields a pixel-level map of crack localizations in the painting.

</details>


### [103] [CRAG: Can 3D Generative Models Help 3D Assembly?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22629)
*Zeyu Jiang,Sihang Li,Siqi Tan,Chenyang Xu,Juexiao Zhang,Julia Galway-Witham,Xue Wang,Scott A. Williams,Radu Iovita,Chen Feng,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CRAG方法，将3D装配重新定义为装配与生成的联合问题，通过相互增强的结构先验和形状上下文，同时生成完整形状并预测部件姿态，在多种复杂场景下达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D装配方法仅将其视为纯姿态估计问题，通过刚性变换重排观察到的部件，而人类装配天然地将结构推理与整体形状推断相结合。受此启发，作者希望将装配与生成两个过程联合建模，使其相互促进。

Method: 提出CRAG（Co-assembly and Re-generation），将3D装配重新表述为装配与生成的联合问题。装配过程为生成提供部件级结构先验，生成过程为装配注入整体形状上下文以消除歧义。模型同时生成合理的完整形状并预测输入部件的姿态。

Result: 在具有多样化几何形状、不同部件数量和缺失部件的真实世界物体上，达到最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 装配与生成是相互增强的过程，联合建模可以同时处理完整和部分缺失的输入，优于仅做姿态估计的传统方法。

Abstract: Most existing 3D assembly methods treat the problem as pure pose estimation, rearranging observed parts via rigid transformations. In contrast, human assembly naturally couples structural reasoning with holistic shape inference. Inspired by this intuition, we reformulate 3D assembly as a joint problem of assembly and generation. We show that these two processes are mutually reinforcing: assembly provides part-level structural priors for generation, while generation injects holistic shape context that resolves ambiguities in assembly. Unlike prior methods that cannot synthesize missing geometry, we propose CRAG, which simultaneously generates plausible complete shapes and predicts poses for input parts.
